  std::ostringstream s;s<<\"CEWSEGMENT/1\\nsegment \"<<m.id<<' '<<m.first<<' '<<m.last<<' '<<m.bytes\n      <<\"\\nchain \"<<m.previous<<' '<<m.last_hash<<' '<<m.previous_segment<<\"\\nbody 2 : 1, kMaxCellState);\n      if (detail::neuron(c.kind)) {\n        c.neurite_proxy = detail::bounded_add(c.neurite_proxy,\n                                                guidance > 0 ? 2 : 1, kMaxCellState);\n        c.rna_proxy = detail::bounded_add(c.rna_proxy,\n                                            rna > 0 ?
